# TRACE Built Inventory
# Flat catalog of every major capability built across all TRACE repos
# Read this before starting any build session — the thing you're about to build may already exist

> 📐 **CAMPAIGN LIFECYCLE — SCOPING + ESTIMATE (2026-08-23, ledger #192/#192b).** NOT a capability entry: **nothing was built.** A scoping document answering David's *"I can add but not edit or cancel or delete"* with nine questions, ten site keys, a proven F1/F2/F3 dependency order, three scopes (**MINIMUM ~2–3 · COHERENT ~5–7 · COMPLETE ~11–14 Thunder prompts; MINIMUM and COHERENT need ZERO migrations**) and eight rulings owed. Read it before ANY campaign build — it is the answer to "has this been scoped?" and its first finding is that the EDIT FORM ALREADY EXISTS as the create form (`Campaigns.tsx:120-198`). → [`docs/audits/campaign-lifecycle-scoping-2026-08-23.md`](audits/campaign-lifecycle-scoping-2026-08-23.md) · predecessor [`social-campaign-path-recon-2026-08-22.md`](audits/social-campaign-path-recon-2026-08-22.md)
**Purpose:** Sessions keep rebuilding things that exist. This document is the single answer to "was X ever built?" Organized by capability, not by file. For file locations, see PLATFORM_AUDIT.md.

> ↑ **FEEDER doc.** The canonical live-status front-page is `TRACE-SESSION-BOOTSTRAP.md` → ⚡ ACTIVE STATUS / 📋 board. This answers "was X *ever* built?"; the front-page answers "what's in-flight / demo-critical *now*." Update the front-page line whenever an entry here changes state.

**Convention:** This is a LEAN index. Each entry = capability + status + one-line location + POINTER to the authoritative doc for detail. Never inline audit/spec content here — link to it. Keep this file scannable.

> **Sibling register (different axis):** `docs/spine-register.md` answers *"is this agnostic capability correctly on the SHARED SPINE, or at risk of per-vertical rebuild?"* — the spine-vs-vertical status axis for [[D-31]] (platform DB + spine-first). This doc answers *"was X ever built?"*; that one answers *"is X correctly shared?"*

> **🆕 Self-device-handoff via QR (2026-07-06, ledger #91 — BUILDER-COMPLETE, migration GATED, owner-proof owed):** an already-authenticated member (owner/manager/staff) generates a short-lived QR from their OWN session on `/profile` ("Add a device"); scanning it on a new phone/tablet logs that device in AS the same member — no URL/email/password typing — and enrolls it in `member_devices`. DISTINCT from the invite flow (invite CREATES an account; handoff AUTHENTICATES an existing one). Mechanism: `admin.generateLink(magiclink)` server-side → `verifyOtp(token_hash)` client-side = a real passwordless Supabase session. Guardrails (bearer credential): short 15-min TTL · single-use (atomic consume) · issued-only-from-authenticated-session (`mdh_self_insert` RLS WITH CHECK) · device-bound on scan. Shared/agnostic ([[D-31]]): `deviceHandoff.ts` (`issueDeviceHandoff`/`exchangeDeviceHandoff`) + `DeviceHandoffScreen.tsx`; exchange rides `api/members/invite.ts` (`action=handoff-exchange`) → **ZERO new Vercel functions (12/12 held)**. GATED migration `20260706_member_device_handoffs.sql` (David applies as postgres → schema gate owed). Ignition untouched. `[TRACE:HANDOFF]` ON.

**Widget-header standard (binding — partnership doc §15, gated at end-of-session-protocol.md Step 10 + Step 17):** every built artifact (widget·tile·component·module·page·endpoint) carries an in-code HEADER declaring **PURPOSE** (one line) · **DEPENDENCIES** (modules/tables/env/capabilities; cross-vertical `requires X`) · **OUTPUTS** (what it produces/returns/writes). Each entry added below describes an artifact that must carry that header in code — the inventory makes a thing *findable*, the header makes it *knowable*. Verify-before-build is the companion: before building, check this index + grep the codebase; if it exists, extend, don't rebuild. A listing without a header — or a header without a listing — is an incomplete task.

> **⚠️ Header backfill debt (flagged 2026-06-14, Tech Debt #33):** the widget-header standard is now binding going forward, but existing artifacts predate it. Top 3 header-less load-bearing widgets to backfill first: (1) `packages/shared/src/campaigns/generate.ts` — David's referenced "campaign widget", no header; (2) `packages/cultivar-os/src/pages/Campaigns.tsx` — customer-facing campaign widget, no header; (3) `packages/shared/src/discovery/engine.ts` — discovery engine, no header. NOT backfilling all now — flagged as debt; backfill on next touch of each file. See `docs/tech-debt-log.md` #33.

> **Audit reconciliation (2026-06-05):** Three stale bootstrap beliefs corrected. (1) AI Engine is already shared, NOT trapped in Ignition — promote already happened; what remains is unifying a split-brain. (2) RBAC is split — identity/invites shared; enforcement still Ignition-side and duplicated. (3) DataBridge footprint confirmed (~45 files, load-bearing) but characterization disputed: this doc says "localStorage-first, intentionally not shared," bootstrap says "offline-sync engine, promote" — unresolved, see NEEDS DAVID'S CALL.
>
> **File-type reality:** Ignition (`packages/ignition-os/`) is entirely `.jsx`/`.js`, ZERO `.ts`. Shared is `.ts`. Audit Ignition with `--include=*.jsx --include=*.js`, never `.ts`. Ignition's `src/` is empty; code lives at package root + `modules/`.

**Column guide:**
- **Vertical** — `ignition` | `cultivar` | `shared` (`shared` = promoted to the platform shared layer; it's a location, not a vertical)
- **Type** — `tile` (customer-facing dashboard tile/module) | `infrastructure` (plumbing: auth, QR, Supabase types, adapters) | `capability` (backend/feature that isn't a tile and isn't pure plumbing)
- Reading the table: `Vertical:shared` = platform baseline. `Vertical:shared AND Type:tile` = every vertical inherits these tiles for free. See "NEEDS DAVID'S CALL" section at bottom for ambiguous entries.

## AI Engine

**What:** Unified multi-provider AI router. Single interface for all AI tasks across all verticals.  
**Status:** 🪦 BACKEND-DEAD / MODULE-STRANDED — **REMOVAL BLOCKED** (deprecated in place, do NOT delete) · ⚠️ lifecycle below  
**Vertical:** shared | **Type:** capability  

> **Lifecycle (2026-06-21) — DEPRECATED IN PLACE, removal blocked:**
> - **Backend dead, logic has no replacement.** Railway backend is killed; AIEngine's client-side task routing + tier-gating (TRIAL/STARTER/PROFESSIONAL/PREMIER) logic has **NO replacement**. The new shared AI gateway (`capabilities.ts` / `execute.ts`) covers **Cultivar server-side tasks only** — the 9 Ignition tasks and the tier-gating logic are **unique to AIEngine** and live nowhere else.
> - **3 live callers in the active build target `ignition-os`** — deleting now breaks `build:ignition`:
>   - `packages/ignition-os/modules/IgnitionAudit.jsx:16` (import) / `:459` (`AIEngine.auditInvoice`)
>   - `packages/ignition-os/modules/IgnitionCipher.jsx:10` (import) / `:74` (`AIEngine.decodeDTC`)
>   - `packages/ignition-os/modules/PredictiveKey.jsx:16` (import) / `:406` (`AIEngine.suggestPMI`)
>   - barrel re-export `packages/shared/src/index.ts:15-16`
> - **DARK in prod but live code.** No endpoint (`VITE_API_URL` unset) → every `AIEngine.call()` returns `{ ok: false }`. Features are dark, but the code is wired and compiled into `build:ignition`.
> - **Stale `gemini-2.0-flash` ×4** (`AIEngine.ts:35-38`) are **harmless while the backend is dead** — they are never called. They are eliminated on eventual removal, **not before** (see config-ground-truth.md store #4).
> - **REMOVAL UNBLOCKED BY:** porting the 9 Ignition tasks + tier-gating to Vercel serverless functions, then repointing the 3 modules. Until then this entry stays BACKEND-DEAD / MODULE-STRANDED, NOT resolved.
> - **Links:** `MASTER_BRIEF.md:444` (Ignition-AI→Vercel port) · Tech Debt #25 + #12 (`docs/tech-debt-log.md`).

**Location:** `packages/shared/src/ai/AIEngine.ts`  
**Original source:** `CAI/AIEngine.js` (archive — do not edit)  
**Backend:** `CAI/ai_router.py` (FastAPI, Railway) — **LEGACY for web builds.** AIEngine.call() fails gracefully (`{ ok: false }`) when no backend is reachable. Port to Vercel serverless functions when activating AI features. See Tech Debt #12 in CLAUDE.md.  
**Import:** `import AIEngine from '@trace/shared/ai/AIEngine'`

**13 tasks:**

| Task | Provider | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `vin_decode` | Gemini 2.0 Flash | Photo → vehicle info |
| `invoice_scan` | Gemini 2.0 Flash | Photo → line items |
| `label_read` | Gemini 2.0 Flash | Tool/fluid label → spec |
| `part_photo_id` | Gemini 2.0 Flash | Part photo → ID + compatibility |
| `invoice_audit` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Invoice → uncaptured charges flagged |
| `dtc_decode` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | DTC codes → plain-language diagnosis |
| `estimate_draft` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Job description → draft estimate |
| `compliance_check` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Document → DOT/regulatory flags |
| `customer_summary` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | History array → relationship summary |
| `pmi_suggest` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Equipment data → maintenance schedule |
| `predictive_analysis` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Job history → risk patterns |
| `savings_report` | Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Shop history → margin recovery report |
| `voice_transcribe` | OpenAI Whisper | Audio → transcript |
| `parts_nlp` | OpenAI GPT-4o | Free-text parts → structured list |
| `intent_classify` | OpenAI GPT-4o | Customer message → intent category |

**Tier gating:**
- TRIAL: all tasks
- STARTER: none (no-AI tier)
- PROFESSIONAL: 12 tasks
- PREMIER: all tasks

`AIEngine.canUse(task, tier)` → boolean. Call before running any task.

**Convenience wrappers:** `decodeVIN`, `decodeDTC`, `transcribeVoice`, `extractParts`, `readToolLabel`, `suggestPMI`, `auditInvoice`, `draftEstimate`, `savingsReport`

**Haiku fallback:** Pass `options.fallback = true` to retry failed Sonnet calls with Haiku.

**Not yet built:** `SavingsReport.jsx` — React display component for `savings_report` output. API is complete; only display work remains.

**✅ Split-brain resolved (2026-06-05):** The four server-side files (`shared/campaigns/generate.ts`, `shared/discovery/engine.ts`, `shared/discovery/synthesis.ts`, `cultivar-os/api/social/generate-posts.ts`) now route through `packages/shared/src/ai/execute.ts` → `CAPABILITIES` registry → Anthropic SDK directly on Vercel (no Railway). This is the new shared AI gateway — separate from AIEngine.ts, which remains the Ignition-specific router for Railway-backed tasks. AIEngine.ts Ignition consumers (`IgnitionAudit.jsx`, `IgnitionCipher.jsx`, `PredictiveKey.jsx`) are unchanged. See `packages/shared/src/ai/` for gateway implementation.

**⚠️ AIEngine is DARK in Ignition production (Audit 2, 2026-06-06):** `VITE_API_URL` is NOT set in the ignition-os Vercel project. Every `AIEngine.call()` in Ignition OS web production returns `{ ok: false }`. Railway is still running but receives zero traffic from the web build. No Ignition AI feature has ever produced real output for a web user. See Tech Debt #12 (expanded) + `docs/audits/2026-06-06-audits-1-4.md` §2a.

**⚠️ `invoice_scan` has ZERO callers (Audit 2, 2026-06-06):** The `invoice_scan` task in the AIEngine routing table is orphaned — no component, hook, or API handler invokes it anywhere. It was never wired. **Do not confuse with `invoice_audit`:** that IS built (two-stage Gemini→Claude on Railway), a leakage tool that reads OUTGOING invoices to flag uncaptured charges. `invoice_scan` → retire. `invoice_audit` → keep in scope for future Ignition (port to Vercel when Railway is killed). See `docs/audits/2026-06-06-audits-1-4.md` §2b.

**⚠️ VIN OCR is a placeholder (Audit 2, 2026-06-06):** The VIN decode feature resolves to `alert('OCR Scanning initializing...')`. No Gemini vision call is ever made. The Gemini vision pipeline has never been proven end-to-end on Vercel — Receipt Keeper v1 will be the first confirmed live Vercel vision pipeline. See `docs/audits/2026-06-06-audits-1-4.md` §2c.

## FastAPI AI Backend (ai_router.py) — LEGACY

**What:** FastAPI router that handles all actual AI provider API calls.  
**Status:** ⚠️ Legacy — built for the React Native mobile app where API keys couldn't live in the bundle. Now that Ignition OS is a Vercel web app, keys live in Vercel env vars and functions handle them server-side. Railway is not needed.  
**Vertical:** ignition | **Type:** infrastructure  
**Location:** `CAI/ai_router.py` (archive)  
**Forward path:** Port the 11 endpoints to TypeScript Vercel functions under `packages/ignition-os/api/`. Start with `dtc_decode` and `estimate_draft` (text-only, no vision complexity). See Tech Debt #12 in CLAUDE.md.  
**Exception:** `voice_transcribe` sends audio files — Vercel's 4.5MB payload limit needs evaluation before porting.

**Endpoints:**
- Gemini: `POST /ai/vin_decode`, `/ai/invoice_scan`, `/ai/label_read`, `/ai/part_photo_id`
- Claude: `POST /ai/dtc_decode`, `/ai/estimate_draft`, `/ai/pmi_suggest`, `/ai/invoice_audit`, `/ai/savings_report`
- OpenAI: `POST /ai/voice_transcribe`, `/ai/parts_nlp`, `/ai/intent_classify`

**Cost tracking:** `_log_usage()` writes to `ai_usage` table (includes `cost_usd` per call).  
**Error tracking:** `_log_error()` writes to `error_events` table. Non-fatal — calls never block.

## Receipt / Expense Storage

**What:** Tables and storage for vendor receipt capture (`receipts`), structured expense records (`expenses`), and owner-declared allocation inputs for cost calculations (`cost_profile`).
**Status:** ✅ BUILT (receipts + bucket, Receipt Keeper v1 = WORKS 2026-06-11) | ❌ expenses + cost_profile NOT YET BUILT
**Vertical:** shared | **Type:** infrastructure

**What is built (2026-06-11):**
- `receipts` table — 14 cols + 6 reconciliation cols. Migrations: `20260612_receipts.sql`, `20260613_receipts_add_line_items.sql`, `20260614_receipts_reconciliation.sql`. All applied to bgobkjcopcxusjsetfob 2026-06-11 (confirmed live test).
- `receipts` Supabase storage bucket — RLS: `20260613_receipts_storage_rls.sql` (3 policies).
- `platform_config` table — `20260611_platform_config.sql`. Holds `ocr_primary_model` + `ocr_fallback_model` (swappable without code change per BENCH-E Rule 7).
- Receipt Keeper v1 UI: `src/pages/ReceiptKeeper.tsx` + extracted modules: `src/utils/receiptReconciliation.ts`, `src/utils/imageCompression.ts`, `src/components/LineItemGrid.tsx`, `src/components/ConflictDialog.tsx`.
- API: `api/receipts/ocr.ts` — Gemini 2.5 Flash primary → Claude Haiku 4.5 fallback → 503.

**Wave 2 — mobile-native invoice capture + infer-then-confirm router (2026-06-20, BUILDER-COMPLETE / owner-proof owed):**
- **Device-aware capture** (`ReceiptKeeper.tsx` `useIsMobile`): MOBILE → camera-first (big "Take Photo", `<input capture="environment">`) + "choose from photos/files" secondary; DESKTOP → drag-drop file upload (no camera). `[TRACE:OCR]`.
- **Relabel** — killed the "Capture truck receipts" Ignition leak. Copy now from `CAPTURE_COPY` (nursery default "Snap a receipt or invoice"); marked for VerticalConfig when that lands.
- **Invoice-shape OCR** (`api/receipts/ocr.ts`): `shape` param (`receipt` default = unchanged for all existing callers | `invoice` new). Invoice prompt is a superset — keeps vendor/date/line_items(+sku)/subtotal/tax/total, ADDS customer_name/phone/email, bill_to + ship_to addresses, due_date, delivery_date. Same provider chain (Gemini→Haiku, platform_config models, image-to-Supabase write). UNKNOWN/null for absent fields (D-9, never 0/fabricated).
- **Review-before-write**: the confirm screen now shows the invoice fields (customer, bill-to/ship-to, due/delivery dates) as editable inputs for human validation before any write.
- **OCR → INVENTORY intake (cap 2.3, NEXT build — reuse-verified 2026-06-25):** recon verdict 🟡 reuse-and-wire (~70% reuse), NOT net-new. The OCR provider chain (`api/receipts/ocr.ts`, writes nothing, `shape:'invoice'` already extracts `line_items[]{description,sku,quantity,unit_price,amount}`) + mobile capture/compress are 100% reusable; the genuine net-new is a small `line_items → business_inventory` mapper + N-row confirm grid + multi-insert (the `business_inventory` insert already reserves `receipt_id`, `BusinessInventory.tsx:154`). No migration needed. Full scope: `docs/decisions/OCR-into-inventory-reuse-verify.md`. **Recon only — build nothing yet.**
- **SOMETIMES-CONNECTED WRITE QUEUE — store-and-forward (`packages/shared/src/sync/`, shared slice, ledger #54 · #143; OWNER-PROVEN by David 2026-08-23 via an airplane-mode test, RECORDED 2026-08-23 by recon #204):** 🔴 **This capability was BUILT, WORKING and UNRECORDED HERE for two months — which is why it was re-questioned the evening of 2026-08-23 and cost a recon to re-establish.** It is entered now so `CORE MANDATE` rule 1 can answer *"do we have offline writes?"* from this file instead of from memory. **WHAT IT IS:** a durable offline outbox — `SyncEngine` (write-through when online, enqueue when not, drain on reconnect), `OfflineQueue` (FIFO, idempotent by `clientId`), `NamespacedStore` (`trace:sync:<businessId>:<domain>:*` over **localStorage**), and the `OfflineOp` envelope (`clientId` · `kind` `insert|update|rpc` · `table` · `payload` · `businessId` · `userId` · `clientTs` · `domain`). **Idempotency without a schema change:** an insert's `clientId` **is** the row PK, so a replay collides on `23505` and is read as already-applied. The `rpc` kind (#143) queues a postgres FUNCTION call so a qty movement and its D-50 ledger row stay ONE transaction whenever it lands — and because `count_reconcile_inventory` computes `delta = counted − current` under a `FOR UPDATE` lock **at apply time**, a queued count reconciles against reality on drain rather than replaying a stale delta (**#57's accumulation class cannot recur here**). Replay triggers: the `online` event · a leftover drain at `start()` · every online `submit()` · a manual **Sync now**. **No polling. `[TRACE:SYNC]` ON.** 🔴 **COVERAGE — TWO SURFACES, AND THAT IS ALL (established from code, recon #204):** `InventoryCount.tsx:194` (domain `inventory-count`) and `AssetCapture.tsx:62` (domain `asset-capture`, paired with the **IndexedDB** `assetBlobStore` for image bytes — a deliberate split, the op queue stays string-clean). **Checkout (scan → picker → Review → submit), the QR plant profile, the delivery route/schedule and the desk reconcile have NO queue.** Corroborated independently: those same two files are the ONLY ones in the app containing any `navigator.onLine` / offline UI. **NO service worker and NO manifest exist anywhere** — so the app shell cannot load offline; store-and-forward works inside an already-open session, and a reload in a dead zone gets nothing. ⚠️ **KNOWN LIMITS, recorded rather than discovered later:** (1) ✅ **FIXED 2026-08-23 (ledger #206, `3d0944b`)** — the resolver in front of the count loop discarded the Supabase error and returned the same `miss` a genuinely-absent tag returns, so an offline scan reported a real tag as *not recognized*. It now returns `ReadResult<T>` and the count sheet names the SERVER while keeping typed entry open; **see the read-honesty entry below.** *The write half was always sound; the read half in front of it was what stopped the loop.* **Cards 2/8 on the offline surface are the owner-prove;** (2) ✅ **FIXED 2026-08-24 (ledger #207, `4056de8`)** — `store.ts` swallowed a quota/disabled-storage failure while the UI reported the count saved. **`save()` now RETURNS its outcome** (`StoreWriteResult`) and the engine reports honestly; **a write-then-read-back probe warns BEFORE the walk.** 🔴 **The online half was worse and is also fixed: it returned `applied` with the database called ZERO times.** ⚠️ **A Safari Private tab still clears the store with the tab — that is the STORE's nature, not a bug, and it is now ANNOUNCED up front rather than discovered; moving off `localStorage` is OWED as S-A.** Cards 1/5 are the owner-prove; (3) `drain()` **breaks on a hard reject and keeps the op**, so a refused write blocks everything behind it permanently — `forget()` has two call sites, both the missing-table case, and **no UI can clear a stuck op**; (4) `clientTs` rides the envelope for reconcile-ordering and **has no reader** in `sync/`; (5) starting a count and minting a brand-new size are **online-only by design**, each refusing with a plain-English reason. **NOT the same mechanism as blind capture (#67):** the drain has **no human in it**, so it reproduces *residual is 0 by construction* on a delay — adjacent substrate, opposite decision axis. **Owner-tests:** `docs/owner-tests/inventory-full-surface-test.md` → `## SURFACE: offline — store-and-forward` (**TEN cards as of ledger #206** — 1, 2, 8, 9, 10 `owed` · 3–7 `needs-test`. **Card 2 was written the same day as EXPECTED-TO-FAIL and is now rewritten to its PASSING form; card 4 is still expected to fail and is deliberately left so — cart persistence was not in scope.**). Recon: `docs/audits/offline-store-and-forward-recon-2026-08-23.md`.
- **WALK-AND-COUNT inventory loop (cap 2.3 — BUILDER-COMPLETE 2026-06-26, ledger #54, owner-proof owed):** the physical-count complement to OCR intake (intake fills the catalog; walk-and-count verifies on-hand). Lauren walks the lot on a phone → scan a plant tag QR → app strips the `/plant/<tag>` URL & resolves the item (never navigates) → enter qty → Save→Next reopens the camera → Complete ends the session. One item at a time, full focus (D-21). **New:** `components/inventory/QrScanner.tsx` (live `getUserMedia`+`jsQR` decode; jsqr chosen over BarcodeDetector — unsupported on iOS Safari, our target, §6 r10 standard-by-value; always-present manual tag-entry fallback) · `pages/InventoryCount.tsx` (`/inventory/count`, under the existing view_costs route gate; idle→scanning→reviewing/unknown→done; resolve order = `cultivar_plants.tag_id` → `business_inventory.sku` → UNKNOWN; Save→Next SETS `business_inventory.qty` AND records the count; UNKNOWN = quick variety/size OR skip-&-flag) · "Start count" entry on `/inventory`. **Record model = GATED migration handed to David (NOT applied):** `supabase/migrations/20260626_inventory_count_sessions.sql` adds `inventory_count_sessions` + `inventory_counts` (durable per-item count: counted_qty, item_label, inventory_id, plant_tag_id, was_unknown, session, timestamp — what RECONCILIATION will later read), RLS owner_all + `is_active_member` member_all (AC-2/AC-3). Deploy-window safe: tables absent → on-hand still updates, count-record skipped with a loud `[TRACE:COUNT]` warning (mirrors deliveries `service_type` fallback). **RECONCILIATION (counted-vs-expected, sold/dead/missing) NOT BUILT** — record model leaves room. NO auth/RLS/existing-table-schema touched. `[TRACE:COUNT]` ON. Verify-first answers homed: `docs/decisions/walk-and-count-inventory-verify-first.md`.
- 🔴 **THE MULTI-PASS WALK IS NOT SUPPORTED, AND THE BOARD ALREADY ASKED FOR IT (cap 2.3 — SCOPED 2026-08-23, ledger #198, NOTHING BUILT):** Lauren counts a block in three passes down one row (3, then 6, then 5). `InventoryCount.tsx:384-390` keys a within-session guard on (variety × size) and the second entry **never reaches the write** — it opens a CONFLICT sheet (`:825-853`) offering **replace or keep and nothing else**, framing her second pass as *"a sale, a miscount, or a tree that moved."* Each replacement re-fires `count_reconcile_inventory`, so the walk ends **`qty = 5`**, three `inventory_counts` rows, three immutable ledger rows, and a summary reading *"3 items counted"* for ONE block. ✅ **The never-add principle IS correctly enforced and is not the defect** — `20260720:528-531` computes `v_delta := p_counted_qty − v_current` and SETs; the delta is **never accepted from the client**; `:510-513` refuses a negative. **The defect is the WORKFLOW, not the arithmetic.** 🔴 **Story gate = CONFLICT: `user_stories.md:424` (`STATUS: written`) lists *"location-spanning tallies (same variety here + in the greenhouse, SUMMED)"* — the board already answers her, and the code built the neighbouring *"last value wins"* clause.** ✅ **Sizing: MINIMUM ≈ 3 prompts with ZERO migrations** — there is no unique constraint on `(session_id, inventory_id)` (`20260626:66-81`), so the data model already permits her three entries; **what refuses them is the client.** **BLOCKED on R-A/R-B/R-C (`RULINGS.md` OWED).** Also recorded unfixed on this surface: the conflict sheet's **"first count" label becomes FALSE after the first recount** (`:387` reads `sessionCounts`, which `:564` overwrites — §6 r18's class); `item_count` inflates on any recount (`:691`); and **`'abandoned'` is a declared session state with no writer anywhere.** → [recon](audits/count-session-multi-pass-recon-2026-08-23.md) ✅ **DESIGN CALL RECORDED 2026-08-23 (14) (ledger #205) — the question is ANSWERED in the spec, not just scoped:** `user_stories.md`'s Billy Bob story **already contained BOTH clauses and both are correct** — a **running tally** (*"4, 6, 8, 12, 16" → 16, last value wins*) is one person counting up **in one spot**; a **location-spanning tally** (*same variety here + in the greenhouse*) **SUMS**. **Lauren's walk is the second act at fine grain — same row, different clumps — which is why the code's running-tally reading gets it wrong.** 🔴 **The app cannot know which act she is performing, so it must ASK — and the conflict sheet ALREADY ASKS. It offers REPLACE and KEEP and has NO ADD, so the one act the spec explicitly names is the one it cannot express.** ✅ **R-A answered with it: ONE ledger row at session close carrying whatever she chose; the three passes are session detail in `inventory_counts`, not three ledger events.** ⚠️ **Riding with the build: tech-debt #93** — the sheet's *"first count"* label goes FALSE after the first recount (`InventoryCount.tsx:387` vs `:564`; on her third pass it reads *"first count 6"* when 6 was the second). **Still NOTHING BUILT.**

- **COUNT GROUPING RESOLVED SERVER-SIDE — a STAFF member can finish a walk (cap 2.3 — BUILDER-COMPLETE 2026-08-30, ledger #238, owner-proof owed; ⚠️ migration `20260830c` WRITTEN, NOT APPLIED):** 🔴 **THE DEFECT IT CLOSES WAS SILENT, WHICH IS WHY IT LIVED SO LONG.** `InventoryCount.tsx` finished each scan with two plain UPDATEs writing `variant_group` onto `business_inventory` — a table whose UPDATE policy gates on `inventory:update` (`20260727_rbac_resource_action_flip.sql:72-74`), which **STAFF does not hold**. A PostgREST update RLS refuses matches **zero rows and returns no error**, and `syncEngine.ts:258-259` decides success from `error` alone, so the engine reported `'applied'`. ✏️ **The walk completed; the grouping did not; the cost landed on the NEXT scan**, when `detectSizeCollision` saw a mixed-group family and resolved UNKNOWN — on the D-45/D-46 multi-size path, which at LAWNS is the common case rather than the edge. **WHAT SHIPPED:** `public.count_group_variant_sizes(p_business_id, p_actor_user_id, p_variant_group, p_row_ids uuid[])` — SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, membership-gated by `assert_movement_actor` exactly like `count_reconcile_inventory` and `count_promote_create_inventory` beside it, and **strictly narrower than both**: it sets `variant_group` and nothing else, and **the column list IS the security boundary** (stated at the function, not left to be re-derived). It returns `grouped_count / requested_count / applied / reason`, riding the refusal convention `syncEngine.ts:250-253` already reads — so a grouping that lands on nothing is now reported instead of being indistinguishable from one that worked (**R-12**). The two client call sites collapse to ONE `groupSizes` helper (§6 r8), and the create path's per-row loop becomes a single statement, so a family can no longer be left half-keyed by a walk that stopped between rows; a partial grouping is a **refusal**, because half-keyed IS the mixed-group state the invariant exists to prevent. 🔴 **NO POLICY WAS WIDENED, AND THAT IS ASSERTED IN THE NEGATIVE** — granting STAFF `inventory:update` would widen the first name on tech-debt #124's over-wide list and hand a counter price/qty/status in order to let them group two pot sizes; owner-test **C3** and the RLS proof both check that STAFF *still* cannot write the table directly. **PROOF:** `scripts/rls/count-group-variant-sizes.rls.mjs` (13 assertions — the silent refusal head-on, the RPC fix on the same session, the column boundary, the shortfall report, no-forgery, AC-3 cross-tenant, and MANAGER unchanged in both directions), deliberately OUTSIDE `npm run verify` because that gate must stay offline and deterministic. Owner-tests **C1–C4**; two `covered` cards flipped to `owed` (OP-14 clause 3). ⚠️ **Found while fixing one caller of a wider problem — tech-debt #127: every `SyncEngine.update` in the app has this same blindness, and the zero-row cap sees the wrapper rather than the callers.** → `supabase/migrations/20260830c_count_group_variant_sizes.sql`
- **OWNER-TEST BOARD + THE COVERAGE GATE (process capability — OP-14 proposed, ledger #136, BUILDER-COMPLETE; NO app code):** **"It works" is a CLAIM until someone drove it through the real UI.** OP-4's two bars said so; **nothing made the gap VISIBLE**, so an unproven surface rendered identically to a proven one and the honest answer to *"can I trust this?"* was a feeling rather than a number. **BUILT: (1)** `docs/owner-tests/inventory-full-surface-test.md` — a **STANDING** full-surface test (**27 checks across 8 surfaces**: resolve · count-promote · typed-entry · add-size · manual-crud · grid · order-picker · offline), organized **BY SURFACE** so an untested one cannot hide — STD-017 made physical. Every card carries `STATUS` (`covered`|`owed`|`needs-test`|`blocked`) · `DEVICE` · `COVERS:` (ledger #) · `LAST-PROVEN` · `SIGNAL` (always secondary). Adopted from Lightning's `orders-full-surface-test.md` shape (by-surface + SETUP + TRACE appendix + "if a result surprises you, re-check GATE 0"), plus the reporting discipline the dated script had (results table, what-to-send-back, blast-radius warning, and cards that exist to catch a guard OVER-reaching). **(2)** `owner-tests.html` — a **PURE renderer**, third sibling of `status.html`/`stories.html`: parses the .md live at open-time, holds **ZERO** data, so **there is no board to regenerate and nothing to drift**. Views: *Not proven* (the honest default) · *No test written* · *All*; filters by surface/status/device/**COVERS**; a **trust meter** headline. §6 r16 held: a deliberate CLONE of the established `stories.html` pattern, not a third invented shape. **(3) THE GATE (OP-14):** a build that adds/changes/polishes a surface **must** add, update, or `needs-test`-mark its card; **a moved surface flips `covered`→`owed`** (*a green check on a moved surface is worse than none — it asserts a proof nobody performed*, the exact shape of **D-49's own suite blessing the defect it was written to prevent**); **Thunder may NEVER set `covered`** — Thunder writes the check and sets `owed`, only David's live run flips it (OP-4's *the builder does not grade their own homework*, made mechanical). Homed exactly as its siblings: force in `end-of-session-protocol.md` → **GATE — OWNER-TEST COVERAGE**; pointers at CLAUDE.md §9 + **§1.6 item 11** + close-step **12** (both APPENDED — steps 1–11 keep their numbers because §1.6 item 9 cites "§9 gate 9"; the OP-13 lesson). **STD-011:** the dated `2026-07-16-inventory-count-add-owner-test.md` was **RETIRED and DELETED** into the standing test — two docs answering "how do I prove the blank-size guard?" are two representations of one fact, and **drift is precisely what makes a test unbelievable**; a per-build proof is now a **FILTER** (`COVERS: #135`). **D-9 applied to our own confidence:** `needs-test` is a first-class, RED value — writing the test isn't always this build's job, **recording that it's missing always is**; a known hole is a decision, an unrecorded hole is a lie by omission. **capture=mobile/reconcile=desktop applied to testing:** `DEVICE: phone` cards must be provable **WITHOUT a console** — a check needing DevTools never gets run in a lot. **VERIFIED, NOT ASSERTED:** the renderer's own parser was extracted and run against the real .md — **27 checks · 8 surfaces · 0 bad STATUS · 0 missing tags · 0 empty bodies**. **⚠️ THE BOARD'S FIRST VERDICT WAS ON ITS OWN AUTHOR'S WORK: 19% PROVEN (5/27), and 4 of 8 surfaces ship NO test** — **order-picker READ** (a counted size actually being sellable: *the entire reason D-45 exists*, never proven end-to-end — the highest-value hole on the board), **offline sync** (shipped, load-bearing, never proven; the lot has dead zones), **delete soft/hard** (money-adjacent: the FKs are ON DELETE SET NULL, so a wrong delete **silently blanks a sales line rather than erroring**), and the **frozen-column** grid standard. **Nobody hid any of that. It was never counted.** That is the finding, and it is the case for the gate. §6 r10: adopted at the cheapest form that meets the need — a markdown file + a one-file renderer; **no test framework, no CI, no coverage tool**, because the thing under test is a human driving a phone in a lot. Home: `docs/CLOSE-OUT-LEDGER.md` #136; `docs/DECISIONS.md` OP-14.
- **THE FOUR WAYS A WORKING VARIETY GOT BROKEN THROUGH THE UI (cap 2.3 — ledger #135, implements D-49 + D-46 + STD-011, **NO new decision**, **✅ OWNER-PROVEN 2026-07-17** — David, live, all four cards (blank-size BLOCKED on virgin stub Lacey Oak · SKU `DISC-1003-25G` from suffixed-sibling base · dup-size `15` BLOCKED+named · banner scoped here/elsewhere both directions), SHA `313de44` ⚠️ **never deployed as itself — shipped via `77ffd8e`; tech-debt #60**):** **D-49's invariant was right; three other mint paths did not obey it, and D-49's own `create` branch left half of it to a sheet that did not argue. All four were minted LIVE, through the UI, in the hour after D-49 was owner-proven.** **THE INVARIANT (one statement, four instances):** *any path that mints or fills a row in a variety family leaves that family PICKER-READY BY CONSTRUCTION — every row grouped, every size non-empty and distinct, SKU derived from the family's BASE.* The count path is now the reference implementation. **(1) SIZE REQUIRED (🔴 the RED one — it broke a clean variety with no warning):** a count with the size box EMPTY **SAVED** — `promote — created {size: null, qty: 60}` beside 15/30/45 → re-scan **UNKNOWN**. **Clean at 16:59, broken at 17:03, by the branch that was supposed to be the fix.** The refusal now lives in the shared `resolveCountTarget` and **the sheet ASKS THAT SAME FUNCTION** — one rule, two gates, agreeing by construction rather than by parallel spelling (D-48's shape; §1.6 item 3 — validated before the WRITE, not merely hidden in the UI). Refusal red-borders the field and SAYS WHY (the FIX 5 pattern); never a greyed button with no reason. Required on **BOTH** sheets — recon, not symmetry: the typed UNKNOWN sheet **resolve-before-creates INTO existing families** and always groups what it mints, so every row it mints is in a family by construction; only *Skip & flag* (record-only) needs no size. ⚠️ **It refuses BEFORE the exact-match branch, and that ordering IS the fix:** a blank size **MATCHES** a size-less row (`sameSizeLabel(null,null)` is true), so a stub counted with no size took a plain `update` and became a size-**less LOT** — the Basham's shape, minted one branch over — **and D-49's OWN SUITE ASSERTED THAT AS CORRECT** (`'stub counted with NO size → plain update'`). **The defect was tested IN and blessed: the D-48 scar, verbatim, inside the suite written to prevent this family of bug.** Corrected and flagged as a correction — not a test adjusted to make a fix pass. **(2) DUP-SIZE GUARD on "+ Add size":** live and still in the data — `DISC-1002` + `DISC-1002-15G`, **same group, same size**. That is **ledger #73/#74's CASE 5, carried as THEORETICAL since 2026-06-30 — minted through the UI in under a minute; it is now CONFIRMED-LIVE.** ROOT: D-46's editor enforces **SKU** uniqueness (`DISC-1002-15G` is unique → the guard passed) and never checked **SIZE** uniqueness within the group — **two different facts**: a SKU identifies one sellable UNIT, a `(variant_group, size)` pair identifies one VARIANT (§6 r16: WooCommerce/Shopify refuse a duplicate option combination *independently* of SKU). FIX: new shared **`findSizeTwin`** beside `findDuplicateSizeGroups` on **ONE key** (`sizeGroupKey`) — same fact, opposite moment (the detector surfaces a collision that exists; the guard refuses to mint one), so guard and detector cannot disagree. Names the twin **and its SKU** and offers to edit that row — never a bare refusal. **(3) SKU BASE (lineage compounded):** live **`DISC-1003-30G-45G`** — "+ Add size" from the `DISC-1003-30G` row handed **that** row's SKU to `deriveSiblingSku`; the next would be `DISC-1003-30G-45G-15G`. **THE HELPER IS NOT THE BUG — THE CALLER IS**, proven by David's own trail: the count path called the SAME helper minutes later on this SAME family and produced **`DISC-1003-60G` correctly**, because it resolved the base first. The rule was **already** a named shared fn (`baseSkuOf` — shortest non-blank SKU) but homed in `countPromote.ts`, which the editor has no business importing; **MOVED to `variantGroup.ts` beside `deriveSiblingSku`** (SKU lineage is a SKU fact, not a counting fact) and both paths now make the identical **`suggestSiblingSku(family, size)`** call. `deriveSiblingSku`'s idempotence guard only ever caught re-appending the *same* suffix — now documented at the helper, since it cannot detect a wrong base. **(4) BANNER SCOPED TO THE VIEW (Surface Honesty inverted):** filtered to "alley" — 4 of 123 shown, all distinct, all grouped, **zero collisions** — and the banner still read *"2 size collisions … Edit the size or variant group on a flagged row to fix it"*: **Acoma's** collision, elsewhere, rendered over four clean rows, **instructing the owner to edit a flagged row when none was on screen**. **D-9 INVERTED — it does not fabricate a value, it MIS-ATTRIBUTES a real one.** FIX: `inView` and `elsewhere` are **two separate honest facts**; a filtered-clean screen gets *"N flagged rows **elsewhere** … nothing on this screen is affected."* **The row FLAG stays catalog-wide on purpose** (a collision is a fact about the data, not the filter — a row must not stop being flagged because its twin scrolled out of view); **only the COUNT is view-scoped, and that distinction is the fix.** The rule was extracted **pure** (`flagCounts.ts`) because it was **unreachable inside a `useMemo`** — the D-49 lesson, one layer up. FOLD-IN: the copy said "2 collisions" while the trace said `Array(1)` — **ONE collision, TWO rows**; copy and trace now count the same noun. **ALSO — `[TRACE:RESOLVE]` told TWO lies, both fixed:** the false `MISS` firing one line after `AMBIGUOUS` (there **were** matches), **and** a **hardcoded `(ungrouped siblings)` cause appended to every ambiguous miss** — Alley Cat's four rows were **all grouped**, so the emit named the wrong cause *confidently*, and the real diagnosis (*blank size on 1 row*) was a **human inference, not the trace's output**. Now **ONE emit per miss** that **SHOWS the candidates** `{size, group, sku}` rather than asserting a cause — the blank size becomes visible **without re-spelling `detectSizeCollision`'s predicate** (a second copy written only to explain the first is exactly what drifts). The resolver's ambiguous miss carries `candidates` (additive). **§6 r8 CONSOLIDATION (reported per the rule):** the **FIX 5 required-field pattern extracted to ONE home** (`packages/shared/src/components/FieldError.tsx`) — the rule of three was **already exceeded**: `errBorder` in **3 byte-identical copies**, `FieldError` in **2**, plus **4 hand-inlined `<p>` bodies**, and **Discounts' own comment pointed at Settings as "the reference" while holding a copy**. All four consumers repointed. `BusinessInventory`'s local `dupKey` retired onto `sizeGroupKey`; the editor's `existingSkus` prop replaced by ONE `peers` prop from which it derives **both** guards — **a caller can no longer supply one guard's data and forget the other's, which is exactly how the size guard came to be missing while the SKU guard shipped.** **⚠️ An un-briefed defect found by my own RED check failing legitimately:** `sizeGroupKey`'s comment *"'||' separator → no cross-pair key collision"* was **FALSE since #74** — `('a||b','c')` and `('a','b||c')` both produced `a||b||c`, and `variant_group` is **owner-editable free text on the grid**, so it is reachable. Mild consequence (a false FLAG, never a wrong write) but **a key that can be forged is not a key**; now a JSON pair, and the comment describes what the code does. **`detectSizeCollision` DELIBERATELY NOT TOUCHED** — it refused to guess on a blank size and on a mixed group and was **RIGHT both times**; that refusal is D-9 working and **it is what surfaced all four defects**. We fixed the paths that CREATE the states it refuses, not the refusal. **STD-002 RED→GREEN, all three suites, against the REAL defects** (the shipped rules were ported verbatim into the new pure modules FIRST — D-49's proven shape): **countPromote 36/16-fail → 52/0 · dupSize 16/6-fail → 22/0 · flagCounts 6/6-fail → 12/0**. Full suite **16/16, zero regressions**. `verify` exit 0 zero net-new (tsc 5 / eslint 248 / knip 10·14·15); `build:cultivar` clean 2353 modules. `[TRACE:*]` ON, **net +2 emits, none silenced**. **DEPLOYED-bar signal: a blank-size save being BLOCKED** + `[TRACE:INVENTORY] promote — REFUSED at the sheet: size-required`. **If a blank-size save still SUCCEEDS you are testing OLD code** — hard-refresh, confirm Vercel READY, do NOT declare pass/fail (#128/#129 scar). **⛔ The #133 fold SQL is STALE — NOT applied, NOT offered to David, and the file itself is now banner-marked ⛔ STALE** (written against 118 rows; catalog at **123**, moved **8×** during the owner-prove; scope GROWN — four orphan pairs + Acoma's CASE-5 twin + `DISC-1003-30G-45G`). **Regenerated against a SETTLED catalog AFTER these fixes are owner-proven.** Its G1-G5 guards + its ORDERING lesson (repoint `inventory_counts` BEFORE the fold — the FK is ON DELETE SET NULL, so deleting first silently orphans the count rather than erroring) are the template. **FLAGGED not built:** tech-debt **#56** (size VOCABULARY — the last live defect of this family; can MERGE rows so it needs a read-only blast-radius probe first) · **#58** (partial unique index `(business_id, variant_group, size)` — #74's option C; **would REJECT the live Acoma dup, so it cannot land until the data is clean**; sibling of #54) · **#59** (bootstrap header, loaded every session) · `findDuplicateSizeGroups` still blind to blank-size rows (asserted as a KNOWN BLIND SPOT in the new suite so it is visible rather than assumed handled). Home: `docs/CLOSE-OUT-LEDGER.md` #135.
- **THE COUNT FILLS THE STUB — counting a variety made it PERMANENTLY UNSCANNABLE (cap 2.3 — D-49 proposed, tech-debt #57 CLOSED / #56 recorded, ledger #133, **✅ OWNER-PROVEN — David, live 2026-07-16, BOTH branches** (`92136b3`): STUB-FILL Bur Oak DISC-1016 → `promote — filled`, 119 rows in / 119 out, SKU intact, re-scan RESOLVES; CREATE Alley Cat → `promote — created`, SKU **DISC-1003-60G** derived from the PARENT even with suffixed siblings present, group inherited, family left picker-ready):** the walk-and-count promote (`InventoryCount.commitCount`) matched on `(variety × size)` and treated a size-**less** parent as "a different size": the scan RESOLVED to the scraped parent at L4 and then **CREATED a second row beside it** (`[TRACE:INVENTORY] promote — created`; inventory **114 → 118** on four scans). The **re-scan of the same slug then returned UNKNOWN** — two token-equal rows, one grouped + one not, one sized + one not → `detectSizeCollision` correctly refuses to guess. **NET: counting a variety made it permanently unscannable** — self-inflicting, once per variety, on the loop the capability exists for. **WHY NOW: #55's owner-prove made this branch reachable for the first time** — before the apostrophe elide those varieties never resolved at L4 at all, so the promote had never been exercised on a size-less parent. *A fix that unblocks a path exposes that path's first real test.* **ROOT:** `populate.ts` mints the scraped catalog as size-less `qty: 0` stubs under the D-9 honesty contract (scrape-reads-variations was never built) — **measured live (read-only, David): 119 rows · 103 stubs · 112 DISC- · 103 DISC- stubs · 5 ungrouped-sized ⇒ 108 of 119 rows would break on first count.** **WHY NOTHING CAUGHT IT:** the suite was green — the decision lived **inline in a React async handler** (unreachable by any test), and the grid's own dup detector `findDuplicateSizeGroups` is **blind to this class** (it skips blank-size rows at `dupSize.ts:44`; the parent's size is blank), so the one surface built to surface this damage could not see it. **FIX = ONE INVARIANT, TWO BRANCHES** — *any path that mints a size-sibling must leave the family in a state where the size-picker fires **BY CONSTRUCTION*** (non-blank `variant_group` on EVERY row, distinct non-empty size on EVERY row, SKU lineage from the family's base — exactly `detectSizeCollision`'s contract): **(1)** a counted **STUB** (qty 0 + no size + no group) is **FILLED in place**, never sibling'd — **this ENFORCES D-34, it does not bend it** (the lot IS the SKU and qty is the COUNT, so a row with no size AND no stock AND no size-family cannot be a lot; if the stub cannot be a lot, the first real lot must BECOME it — the filled row keeps its `DISC-####` SKU + scrape lineage); **(2)** an **UNGROUPED NON-STUB** (group null, size present — 5 live rows, e.g. Flip Side Vitex DISC-1104) **creates a real sibling AND auto-groups the parent** in the same pass — **D-46's own rule (#126) finally reaching the SECOND path that mints siblings** (`commitCount` backfilled `variant_group` on the MATCH branch only). **CREATE is not the defect — CREATE-while-leaving-the-parent-ungrouped is**, proven by live data: `'Sierra' Mexican Red Oak` and `Arizona Cypress Blue Ice` were minted by this SAME count-promote CREATE (their siblings' `sku NULL` is that path's fingerprint) and **both work today**, because their parents already carried a group. **BUILT:** the decision extracted **PURE** to `packages/shared/src/inventory/countPromote.ts` (D-47's proven shape — the decision lives in shared and is testable without a DB; the IO stays in the caller), exporting the ONE canonical `isVarietyStub` predicate (STD-011 — never re-spelled inline), `sameSizeLabel` (the local `sameSize` copy RETIRED), `baseSkuOf`, `resolveCountTarget`; SKU lineage repointed onto the SHARED `deriveSiblingSku` (the STD-011 drift being fixed: two paths minted a sibling, one derived the SKU — which is why the four counted rows have no SKU); `[TRACE:INVENTORY] promote — filled | created | auto-grouped parent`. **`detectSizeCollision` DELIBERATELY UNTOUCHED** — refusing to guess is *correct* and is what stopped a bad merge; the fix stops MANUFACTURING the ambiguous pair. **STD-002 RED-first, honestly:** the CURRENT shipped logic was first ported verbatim into the new pure module so the RED landed against the **real** defect rather than a strawman → **`24 passed / 16 failed` (exit 1)** → **`40 / 0`**; every fixture is real live data; the re-scan assertions call the **REAL shipped `detectSizeCollision`** (the #55 lesson: verify against the real function, never the test's copy); **no existing assertion adjusted**; the `'Sierra' UNREGRESSED` control **passes even in RED**. ⚠️ **STD-001 — the live probe was BLOCKED and SURFACED, not guessed:** `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` is empty in every local env file and anon is correctly RLS-blocked, so Thunder **refused to write the DELETE against unverified ids**; David ran the read-only query — **and was right to be asked: the prompt said 114→118, the catalog said 119→115, and "~113 stubs" was an inference (measured 103)** — the third consecutive prompt row-count error after 111→116 on #55. **DATA REMEDIATION:** ONE gated SQL (`docs/decisions/2026-07-16-d49-stub-fold-remediation.sql`) folds the 4 orphan children into their parents — **order is load-bearing** (`inventory_counts.inventory_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL, so the repoint precedes the delete or the count record is silently orphaned); one transaction, five abort guards (G1–G5, incl. **proving** zero `order_items` refs rather than assuming), every value COPIED FROM THE CHILD ROW never retyped, `status` untouched (mirrors the code's `fill` branch). **PART 3 fenced + commented-out + independent:** SKU backfill for the two REAL size-siblings (`'Sierra'` 30 gal → `DISC-1001-30G`, `Arizona` 30 → `DISC-1005-30G`) — **not folded, they are genuine second sizes under grouped parents and they work**. **Scope held: the 103 stubs + 5 ungrouped rows get NO SQL** — the code fix makes their first count correct. **NO schema migration** (size/variant_group/sku all exist), **ZERO api-fn (12/12, counted)**. verify exit 0 zero net-new (tsc 5 / eslint 248 / knip 10·14·15); - **APOSTROPHE ELIDE in the L4 name-token resolver — the possessive false-UNKNOWN (cap 2.3 — tech-debt #55 CLOSED 2026-07-16, ledger #132, implements D-45, NO new decision, ✅ OWNER-PROVEN 2026-07-16, SHA `d99e829` — all four possessive varieties resolve at L4 on the first scan, `key:` correct, `afire` intact; ⚠️ its owner-prove is what EXPOSED tech-debt #57 / D-49, ledger #133 — the fix made a never-fired D-45 branch reachable):** `nameTokenSet` (`packages/shared/src/utils/canonicalName.ts` — the #61 L4 key that BOTH the scan resolve and the `populate` import key through) treated the apostrophe as a token BOUNDARY, and the 1-char filter — which exists to kill the botanical hybrid marker `x` — then ate the orphaned fragment. `"Basham's Party Pink Crape Myrtle"` → `{basham,party,pink,crape,myrtle}` vs its own WordPress slug `bashams-party-pink-crape-myrtle` → `{bashams,…}` ⇒ **NO MATCH → false-UNKNOWN at scan**; `"Hearts A'fire Redbud"` → `{hearts,fire}` — the `a` eaten outright. **4 of 6 apostrophe varieties in the LIVE catalog were unscannable** (Basham's Party Pink Crape Myrtle `91be4388…` DISC-1009 · Evey's Pride Mimosa `970aa781…` DISC-1070 · Summer's Tower Redbud `076a20a1…` DISC-1099 · Hearts A'fire Redbud `518dd451…` DISC-1091 — all `variant_group` NULL, never counted). **WHY IT HID:** WRAPPING quotes survive either way (`'Sierra'` → `{sierra}` — the quotes sit at word boundaries), and the whole D-45/D-46 size-variant arc was owner-proven on `'Sierra' Mexican Red Oak`: **the one case anybody eyeballs works.** It was already flagged in prose at `personName.ts:27-30` during D-47 and correctly deferred as owner-proven territory needing its own provable build. **FIX (3 files):** ELIDE the apostrophe **BEFORE** the non-alnum boundary split — **order of operations IS the fix** (split first and `A'fire` → `a`+`fire` → the 1-char filter eats the `a` → `{fire}`); codepoints **U+0027 · U+2019 · U+02BC · U+0060 · U+00B4** (live data is U+0027-only, probed, but the scrape source is WordPress which emits U+2019 — a curly-apostrophe miss is the same bug in another codepoint); **the 1-char filter STAYS** (hybrid-`x` reason holds; eliding merely stops feeding it orphans). `canonicalName.test.ts` +13 assertions; `InventoryCount.tsx` `[TRACE:RESOLVE]` now logs the token set on the L4 hit **and the MISS** (a false-UNKNOWN was previously invisible in the trail). **§6 r16 deviation STATED:** Lucene STRIPS the possessive (`basham`), we ELIDE (`bashams`) — the comparison target is a WordPress `sanitize_title` slug, so stripping would still miss. **STD-002 RED-first:** `21 passed / 4 failed` (exit 1) → `34 passed / 0 failed`; no existing assertion adjusted. **BLAST RADIUS probed LIVE read-only BEFORE the fix (STD-001 — `populate.ts:203-206` is a WRITE path): 116 rows (NOT the 111 previously cited — flagged) · exactly 4 token sets change · L4 pairwise 0 un-grouped / 0 re-grouped · populate.ts import 4 FIXED / 0 BROKEN · 0 groups merging distinct varieties** ⇒ no working grouping moved, no STOP condition. `personName.ts` UNTOUCHED and still a separate fn (two facts, two rules — surfaced under §6 r10, deliberately NOT merged; they share only the ONE `tokenSetsEqual` engine). **NO migration**, **ZERO api-fn (12/12)**. verify exit 0 zero net-new (tsc 5 / eslint 248 / knip 10·14·15); `build:cultivar` clean 2350 modules; canonicalName 34/34 · customerIdentity 22/22 (D-47 unregressed) · catalog-variants 31/31. **FLAGGED not built:** `variant_group` backfill for the four (a separate D-45 concern — the scan RESOLVES without it since each is one row → `matches.length===1`; the group only matters when a 2nd size is added).
- **UNITS OF MEASURE — WHAT A QUANTITY ON `business_inventory` ACTUALLY MEANS (cap 2.3 / 5.1 — ledger #234, R-27, 2026-08-30, **BUILDER-COMPLETE, migration WRITTEN + NOT APPLIED, 0 of 5 owner-test cards**):** `size` is free text and 20260628's own header says it was scoped to container flavours (*"container gallons / caliper inches / height"*); LAWNS's catalogue carries **six families** — container, volume (yard scoops), weight (bags/bottles), length (by the roll), each/kit (T-posts). **The forcing case: Fertile Compost Mix sells as a 15/30/45 gallon BUCKET *and* a half-yard and full-yard SCOOP** (FCMB15/30/45 + SFCM1/SFCM2) — one pile, five sale units, ~13 buckets to the yard. **SHIPPED:** `20260830_inventory_unit_of_measure.sql` — five NULLABLE columns (`unit_kind` · `unit_value` · `unit_value_max` · `unit_name` · `unit_parsed_from`), a **NAMED** CHECK over the closed five-kind taxonomy, a **NAMED** projection-invariant CHECK, and a BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE guard; `packages/shared/src/inventory/unitOfMeasure.ts` — **the ONE parser** (`parseUnitOfMeasure` · `unitColumnsFor` · `withUnitColumns` · `findMultiUnitGroups` · `summariseUnits`), 166 assertions over David's 30-string live corpus; `scripts/verify-unit-projection.mjs` — a **new cap in `npm run verify`**; `scripts/backfill-inventory-units.ts` — re-runnable, per-tenant, with a read-only `--verify` mode. Wired at three write sites (`inventoryEdit.ts` ×2 — the chokepoint for the grid, the editor and the import setLot; `importWrites.ts` create; `populate.ts`). 🔴 **NOT ONE OF THE 25 READERS OF `size` CHANGED — 24 of 24 reader/decider files proven UNCHANGED by diff.** `sizeGroupKey`, `detectSizeCollision`, `normalizeSize`, `skuSizeSuffix`, `LARGE_CONTAINERS` and the QuickBooks description line are byte-identical. 🔴 **R-27 — the columns are a PROJECTION, never a parallel truth:** derived on every write, editable nowhere (all five in `SYSTEM_MANAGED_FIELDS`), and `unit_parsed_from` proves which string they describe. **Either correct or ABSENT, never stale** — which is what let this land without touching the count screen's SECURITY DEFINER RPCs. ✅ **A range stays a range**; ✅ **unparseable is NULL and LISTED BY VALUE** (`3GP`/`1DP`/`2DP` refuse rather than guess). ⚠️ **NOT this build:** conversion · product grouping · stock in a base unit — conversion needs Lauren's answer to *is compost stocked in yards or in buckets*. ⚠️ **`normalizeSize`'s four measured defects → tech-debt #125, filed not fixed, each with a negative control so they cannot be laundered into the new column.** → `user_stories.md` *A quantity that means something*
  **✅ APPLIED + BACKFILLED 2026-08-30 (ledger #236).** Migration verified (A)–(G), **(F) proved the guard lets go**. Backfill: **578 rows read / 478 written / 0 failed / 0 disagreements** across 3 tenants — LAWNS 447 parsed, Test Dave's 31 parsed (99 carry no size), third tenant 1 row no size. 🔴 **LAWNS's unparsed list is EMPTY and it is real: all 447 rows are `unit_kind = container`.** `3GP`/`1DP`/`2DP` are not in this table at all — every non-container family is in QuickBooks, so **the refusals arrive with the 685-item import.** 🔴 **What the read found instead: 15 physical sizes spelled 46 ways, SIX of 198 families unscannable, and `skyward-holly` invisible to the dup-size flag (`"5 gallon"` vs `"5G"`) → tech-debt #126**; and #125's ranges are live on four named rows.
- **RECONCILE SCREEN — the count becomes stamped truth (cap 2.3 / cap 5.1 — D-50 BUILD 2B, ledger #145, 2026-07-21, **🟢 OWNER-PROVEN 2026-07-22**):** **✅ OWNER-PROVEN 2026-07-22** (David, live — SHAs `6245f27`+`679fb9a`, tenant `f7ec5d67`, lot `3ec53db3` Shoal Creek Vitex 45; evidence is the DB, not assertion). **All 9 cards green.** Baseline: book **60** → counted **58** wrote **ONE** `count_reconcile`, delta **−2**, actor `95c1b2e9`, reason carried, qty→**58**. **RECEIVE:** a +5 desk edit was absorbed into expected — residual **0**, itemized *"5 adjusted by hand"*, **not phantom shrinkage** — CORRECTION (A) held in the field. **ATTRIBUTION-SPLIT:** **−17** partitioned into `dead −4` + `loss −3` + `count_reconcile −10` = exactly **−17**, landing on **46**, **not −24** — CORRECTION (B) held; the seven invented dead plants did not happen. A zero-delta `count_reconcile` on agreement wrote correctly; the unrecognized-scan queue rendered 3 scans with honest copy and no fake Resolve button; **V3(b) `qty == SUM(delta)` returned ZERO rows** with every new reconcile write counted in the sum. **⚠️ A REPLAY-DISPLAY DEFECT WAS FOUND, FIXED AND RE-PROVEN IN THE SAME PASS (`679fb9a`, ledger #146):** the screen read *"replays to 120 but book says 60"* against a true replay of 58. **The reported root cause was WRONG** — not a table+view double-read (there is exactly ONE read); the D-50 genesis backfill dates every `opening_balance` at migration-apply time, so the genesis row fell INSIDE the window and was replayed as fresh arrival. `isMovement()` now distinguishes a **position-assertion** from a **change**. Re-proven live: replay **46** == book **46**, banner gone.  **the desk surface that turns a physical count into an append-only, dated, actor-stamped ledger event.** NEW route `/inventory/reconcile` (`src/pages/InventoryReconcile.tsx`, VIEW_COSTS gate — the same group as `/inventory` and `/inventory/count`, deliberately: a second, looser door onto the same stock is the gap route-entry enforcement exists to close) + NEW **pure** decision module `src/lib/reconcileMath.ts` + `reconcileMath.test.ts` (**48/0**). Extracted pure for the same reason `countPromote.ts` was (D-49): **arithmetic that decides what gets written to an append-only log must be testable without a database, because a wrong row cannot be retracted.** MODE IS AUTOMATIC PER LOT — no prior `inventory_counts` row → **BASELINE** (the count IS the truth; no attribution is asked because there is no history to explain a first count against, and the "since last count" column is **ABSENT, not empty** — an empty sold-column on a fresh tenant reads as a broken feature on the screen meant to prove how easy this is); prior count → **DELTA** (the ledger window replays as EVIDENCE, and a nonzero residual demands a human account). ACCEPT writes through the LAYER 1 RPCs only (`adjust_inventory_manual` per attributed cause, then the closing `count_reconcile_inventory`) — **the page performs no direct qty UPDATE**, and **nothing is written until a human presses Accept.** Reads BASE ledger columns only (`kind`, not `event_type`/`aggregate_type`) because `20260720_ledger_event_store_columns.sql` is still **GATED** — selecting a column that may not exist live would break the page on a deploy the owner has not applied. Reserved/available come from the ONE D-52 derivation (`fetchCommittedByLot`/`availableFrom` in `inventoryStates.ts`, imported never re-exported). `[TRACE:RECONCILE]` ON by birth on load/math/accept/every RPC step. **⚠️ TWO CORRECTIONS TO THE BUILD SPEC, both proven by mutation test (reverting either fails the suite):** **(A)** `expected` is a **full replay of ALL deltas in the window**, not the spec's `prior_count − sales` — the ledger records receive · dead · loss · found · adjust · tombstone · rescan_clear too, so subtracting sales alone turns a **+10 receive into a −10 residual** and asks a human to account, permanently and by name, for a movement the system itself recorded (D-9 inverted — it mis-attributes a REAL movement rather than fabricating one). D-50 LAYER 1 owner-proved `qty == SUM(delta)` with zero drift, so the correct replay lands on book on-hand by construction; **both are computed and compared** (`bookAgreesWithReplay`) and a disagreement is SURFACED, because that would mean the ledger's own guarantee has broken. **(B)** attribution **SPLITS** the delta instead of appending a second one — `count_reconcile_inventory` AND `adjust_inventory_manual` **both move qty**, so the spec's "count_reconcile for the change, then a dead/loss adjust per cause" **decrements twice**: book 30 → counted 13 with 4 dead + 3 lost landed on **6**, net **−24 instead of −17** — *seven invented dead plants in a log that cannot be retracted.* Each attributed cause now writes its own qty-moving event and the **unexplained remainder** lands on the closing `count_reconcile`, so the steps sum to exactly `counted − book` and every cause gets its own permanent dated line (which is what D-50's story literally asks for — "4 dead, 3 unexplained loss"). Order is load-bearing twice: **credits before debits** (a −6 dead before a +4 found on a lot of 3 would RAISE and strand half an attribution in an append-only log), and the **closing step lands ABSOLUTELY on `counted`** so a concurrent sale mid-plan is absorbed rather than lost. `miscount` is deliberately NOT an attribution kind — "the book was wrong, nothing physically happened" is exactly what a `count_reconcile` row already means, so it is the REMAINDER, not a second row asserting a movement that did not occur. **Zero api-fn (12/12 — the RPCs are postgres functions and the reads run under existing RLS), zero schema, zero migration.** **⚠️ WHAT IS *NOT* BUILT — three, all named rather than swept:** **(1) BLIND CAPTURE (tech-debt #67)** — `InventoryCount.tsx:438` already calls `count_reconcile_inventory` **at capture time**, so a phone count applies itself and a desk reconcile *of that walk* reads residual **0 by construction**; DELTA mode is real and provable for movements landing **after** a count, but it is **not** the count-then-review loop D-50's story describes. `blind_capture_mode` is named in that story's own `PIECES:` and is **BUILD INPUT (2), explicitly owed to David**. **(2) SESSION SCOPING (tech-debt #68)** — the spec asked for it; blocked by #67 (a session view would render a screen of "agrees — done" proving nothing) and by `inventory_count_sessions.status` already having one owner. **(3) UNKNOWN-SCAN RESOLUTION (in-screen)** — the `was_unknown` queue READS (the reader those rows never had) but offers **no Resolve button**, because a control that looks actionable and isn't is a dead affordance (§1.6 item 5); the card says so plainly. → owner-tests `## SURFACE: reconcile` (**9 cards, all `covered` 2026-07-22**; ⚠️ three of them — BASELINE-no-column · nothing-written-until-Accept · refuse-below-zero — rest on David's blanket "all cards green" report rather than a cited figure, flagged so the distinction stays visible); story `user_stories.md` "Reconcile the count against the ledger — leakage becomes arithmetic" (ARC asset-inventory-pmi, MAPS-TO 2.3/5.1)

- **INVENTORY MOVEMENT LEDGER — the DB layer (cap 2.3 / cap 5.1 — D-50 Layer 1, ledger #140, migration `20260720_inventory_movement_ledger.sql`, SHA `2caeac7`, ✅ OWNER-PROVEN 2026-07-20 at postgres):** **a quantity can no longer move without a durable row explaining it — enforced by the DATABASE, not by remembering.** LIVE NOW: `business_inventory_ledger` (signed `delta`; `kind` with NO CHECK per AC-4; `inventory_id` **ON DELETE SET NULL** so history outlives the row) · **DB immutability** — `reject_inventory_ledger_mutation` trigger + REVOKE UPDATE/DELETE/**TRUNCATE** incl. `service_role`, **reusing the proven `audit_log` guard pattern verbatim in shape** (`20260623:151-171`) · RLS `owner_all` OR `member_all`, **FOR ALL so SELECT is covered** · **genesis backfill** (one `opening_balance` per lot → replay == on-hand from row one) · **6 SECURITY DEFINER RPCs** funnelling all 14 emit points / 5 chokepoints, each doing the qty write AND the ledger INSERT in ONE transaction (`adjust_inventory_qty` amended backward-compatibly via DROP-and-recreate — an overload would have made `submit.ts`'s 3-named-arg call ambiguous and broken checkout · `count_reconcile_inventory` · `count_promote_create_inventory` · `adjust_inventory_manual` · `soft_delete_inventory` · `discovery_create_inventory`/`discovery_rescan_clear`) · **no hard delete survives** — a lot is TOMBSTONED (status flips, row SURVIVES, qty 0), closing the `inventoryEdit.ts:154` hard-delete that removed stock with no movement row · **re-scan bar** — any lot with non-`opening_balance` ledger history, an `inventory_counts` row, or nonzero qty is SKIPPED AND REPORTED, never wiped · **`audit_log` FIRST APPLICATION-AUTHORED WRITER** (`inventory.delete`) after the vault sat **27 days empty**. Actor validation uses `is_member_of(biz, uid)` on the **PASSED** actor (`is_active_member()` reads `auth.uid()`, NULL under the service key — it would have refused every sale). **⚠️ WHAT IS *NOT* BUILT: LAYER 2.** The ten app-side callers are **UNCHANGED** and do NOT route through these RPCs — **a count still overwrites rather than reconciles**, and `InventoryCount.tsx:612-614`'s live *"count record SKIPPED … inventory already updated"* warning still stands. The DB layer is proven; the app has not been rewired. → `docs/decisions/2026-07-19-inventory-movement-ledger-D50.md` (D-50), `docs/decisions/2026-07-19-qty-write-and-audit-emit-recon.md` (the emit map)
- **Infer-then-confirm router** (`[TRACE:ROUTER]`): after OCR, infers doc type (customer present → "invoice for a customer" else "receipt/expense"), presents destinations multi-select, best-guess pre-checked, always overridable — **Add customer** (functional) + Schedule delivery / Analyze sale (shown, marked "coming"). Question-depth dial-able.

- **Day view:** `DeliverySchedule.tsx` shows a service_type badge (planting=blue, delivery_only=green).
- **Owner-proof owed (on phone, after Vercel deploys `main` ≥634b990):** snap the Marcus Webb invoice → "Schedule delivery" is LIVE (no "coming") → service type shows inferred + correctable → confirm → Marcus Webb under Jun 25, 2026 with the service_type badge → "Route this day" plots.

**CHECKOUT → A SCHEDULED DELIVERY — the second door onto the same table (2026-08-25, ledger #216, BUILDER-COMPLETE / owner-proof owed):** 🔴 **THE GAP THIS CLOSES, and it is worth stating plainly because both halves above read as though it were already true: `orders` and `deliveries` were UNCONNECTED.** Every stop on `/delivery-schedule` and `/deliveries` arrived through the OCR-invoice door (`api/customers/create.ts`) — so **a real order taken at the counter for delivery next Thursday appeared on NEITHER screen**, and the truck's day was assembled from photographed paper only. Verified at source before building: `submit.ts` contained **zero** `deliveries` references, so the write was **ABSENT, not failing** — this is a net-new connection, not a repair. Measured instance: order `9a3cbc8b-db56-49d6-bdd5-c07c9bcd2888` (`transport_method` `install`, `delivery_date` 2026-09-04), present in `orders`, absent from every delivery surface. `[TRACE:DELIVERY]` ON.
- **The write (`api/orders/submit.ts` → `scheduleCheckoutDelivery`, called at step 12b):** ONE `deliveries` row when the order means our truck goes out — `transport_method` of `delivery` or `install`. **`self` writes nothing, and the insert is never even ATTEMPTED** (a no-op that still round-trips would look identical on a row count and be wrong). Columns: `business_id`/`customer_id` from the order · `delivery_date` from the order's own gated column · address from the customer · `status:'scheduled'` · **`source:'checkout'`** (distinguishable from `'ocr-invoice'`) · `notes` = the invoice number (`CLV-…`), the breadcrumb back to the order · `service_type` from **`deliveryServiceType()`**: `install → 'planting'`, `delivery → 'delivery_only'`. **NO third word was invented** — the two are the OCR door's own vocabulary (`ReceiptKeeper.tsx:64-67`, rendered `DeliverySchedule.tsx:66`).
- **The address read is D-41's, not a fifth opinion:** canonical `billing_*` first, the legacy unprefixed four as fallback (`customerUpsert.ts:123-135` writes both together), whitespace-only treated as absent, and **a genuinely absent field stays NULL — never `''`** (A9: absent is not empty). A customer with no address on file **still gets a stop**, visibly blank: the truck still goes out.
- 🔴 **IT CANNOT FAIL THE ORDER, AND THAT IS STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN CONVENTIONAL.** Every failure path returns an outcome object, so nothing the function can produce reaches `handleCreate`'s catch — the order completes, the QuickBooks invoice pushes, the customer is confirmed. §6 r6's reasoning (*an integration failure never blocks an order*) applied to an INTERNAL write. Placed **after every order write has committed and BEFORE the QBO push**, on the push's own documented reasoning: there is no wrapping transaction, so an invocation killed mid-QuickBooks leaves the order whole — and the stop should be whole with it.
- 🔴 **IT PROVES IT WROTE (R-12, 2026-08-23), IN BOTH DIRECTIONS.** A PostgREST insert matching ZERO rows returns **success with no error** — the RLS-refused shape — so `!error` is not evidence. `.select('id')` + **`rows.length !== 1`**: a zero-row insert AND a two-row insert are each refused as failures, with the count named in the log. The two-row half is not decorative — a build guarding only *"is there a first row?"* passes the zero case incidentally and reports success over a multi-row write.
- ✅ **NO permission gate, and the silence is a RULING not an oversight.** The 2026-07-31 *capability-not-a-field* ruling was made **about this exact line** and named `deliveries:create` as the wrong string for it (*"granting it would also hand her the ReceiptKeeper delivery INSERT she was never owed"*); tech-debt #84 closed on the same reasoning. Corroborating fact: the anon QR checkout path carries **no token at all**, so a gate here would not narrow the act — it would delete it.
- **Nothing else moved:** ZERO migrations, ZERO schema, ZERO policy, ZERO new permission string, ZERO new cap, api/ **12/12**. The OCR capture path, existing `deliveries` rows, `/delivery-schedule`'s rendering and `/deliveries`' filter were all left untouched.
- **Proof:** `packages/cultivar-os/api/orders/checkoutDelivery.test.ts` — **55 probes that COUNT ROWS**, before and after, for each of the four cases (delivery +1 · install +1 · self +0 · the write forced to fail +0 and no throw). **RED-FIRST against the real source (STD-024):** four defects planted into `submit.ts` in turn — the count check dropped, the null-guard-only variant, `self` made to schedule, the catch removed, the invoice number dropped — each **failing and named**, source then restored **`diff`-confirmed identical**.
- ⚠️ **TWO KNOWNS SHIPPED BY DECISION, NOT DISCOVERED LATER — tech-debt #108 and #109.** **#108: there is no `order_id` column and this build added none** (David: *"KNOWN AND ACCEPTED, DO NOT SOLVE… Do NOT add a column to prevent it"*), so a re-submitted order can mint a SECOND stop, an edited order's date change **cannot reach its stop** (it stays on the old day), and a deleted order leaves an orphan. **#109: `deliveries` now has a THIRD app write path** and the column mapping is a near-twin of `customers/create.ts:103-114` — BASELINED (*known today*), deliberately **not** declared in `ALLOWED_DIVERGENCE` (*correct forever*), because unlike every declared multi-path table the column overlap here is **total**. `write-paths-baseline.json` `deliveries` 2 → 3 in one explicit line; the table still prints **GOAL:FAIL** so the debt stays visible.
- **Owner-tests:** NEW `docs/owner-tests/checkout-delivery-scheduling-full-surface-test.md` — **7 cards, NONE `covered`** (1–5 `owed`, 6–7 `needs-test` with reasons). CARD 5 is the load-bearing one: **count the stops before and after — +1 / +1 / +0**, not eyeballed. Story: `user_stories.md` § ARC: delivery → *"The order I just rang up is on Thursday's truck"* (**`STATUS: gap`** — written by Thunder as the as-built case; **David owns promoting it**).

**What is NOT yet built:**
- `expenses (id, business_id, receipt_id, source CHECK IN ('receipt','bank_csv','manual'), amount, category, occurred_at)` — structured expense records from all sources
- `cost_profile (id, business_id, home_office_pct, business_time_pct, labor_rate, fixed_overheads jsonb, asset_amortization jsonb)` — owner-declared allocation inputs

All tables anchor to `business_id` — AC-1 ✅, AC-2 via `business_id`-scoped RLS ✅.

**Build path:** ✅ Receipt Keeper v1 done. Business Asset Layer done (2026-06-12). Receipt Keeper v2 + Cost-to-Produce tile adds `expenses` + `cost_profile`. See `docs/audits/2026-06-06-audits-1-4.md` §3.

⚠️ **PENDING REQUIREMENTS — must implement when consent surface / data-entry activation widget is built:**

**REQ-1 — WIDGET CONSENT-TO-USE (REQUIRED):**
When a customer activates the Receipt Keeper data-entry widget (e.g. opens the file picker / initiates data entry), the widget MUST present an upfront consent-to-use surface BEFORE data entry proceeds — consent to use the tool and how their data is handled. This must appear at the moment of activation, not buried in a settings page or terms link. Do NOT build the activation step without this surface.
Code anchor: `// FLAG: REQ-1` in `packages/cultivar-os/src/pages/ReceiptKeeper.tsx` at the idle step entry point.

**REQ-2 — HANDWRITTEN-RECEIPT KNOWN-LIMITATION DISCLOSURE (REQUIRED):**
That same upfront consent surface MUST state clearly that HANDWRITTEN receipts are a known issue and must be carefully inspected by the user before saving — handwriting capture is unreliable. Do not suppress or minimize this limitation.
Evidence (2026-06-11): a handwritten Schrock's A/C invoice read all line items as $0.00, missed the $395 handwritten total, missed a "pd Venmo" payment annotation, and fell to the Claude Haiku fallback. Printed receipts read cleanly. This is not an edge case — it is a known failure mode.
Code anchor: `// FLAG: REQ-2` in `packages/cultivar-os/src/pages/ReceiptKeeper.tsx` at the idle step entry point.

**Framing discipline:** These are CAPTURE/DISCLOSURE requirements — state the limitation and require human inspection. Do NOT add business advice about how to handle receipts or what to do about the limitation. Consistent with TRACE's capture-not-rule line and the Surface Honesty principle.

## Business Asset Layer (Cost-to-Produce)

**What:** Schema + UI layer for tracking business-owned assets and inventory. Foundation for the Cost-to-Produce tile. Part of the same receipt-anchored cost model as Receipt Keeper — receipts can link to inventory rows via the COUNT-ONCE dedup seam.  
**Status:** ✅ WIRED 2026-06-13 — schema applied, manual-entry UIs live, PMI module rewired onto live tables; AI suggest endpoint added. ⚠️ David must run `20260613_business_service_log_result.sql` before using the log result field.  
**Core-1 (2026-06-15 built; APPLIED + catalog-proven 2026-06-16):** `business_assets` → `cost_objects` rename-in-place + node/edge schema + D-5 substantiation axis. Migrations `20260615_cost_objects_rename_and_node_schema.sql` + `20260615_cost_objects_substantiation_d5.sql` applied to live (bgobkjcopcxusjsetfob); `node scripts/verify-cost-objects.mjs` catalog gate 22/22 green. See "Core-1 — Cost-Object Node Model" subsection below.  
**Vertical:** cultivar (UI surfaces today; schema AC-1 clean, business_id-scoped) | **Type:** infrastructure

**What is built (2026-06-12):**

🔴 **AN UNDER-PRODUCTION BLOCK WOULD BE OFFERED FOR SALE — AND NOT BY OMISSION (found 2026-08-23, ledger #196 — RECORDED, NOT FIXED).** The *growing ladder* story says a block spends six to eight months **under production**, not for sale at any price. Checked against the code and **CONFIRMED, with the mechanism stronger than the story states**: `availableFrom(onHand, committed)` is `max(0, onHand − committed)` (`packages/cultivar-os/src/lib/inventoryStates.ts:62-64`) — **two inputs, maturity is not one of them** — and `checkSellable` (`:169-200`) takes exactly four inputs (`onHand`/`committed`/`status`/`sellPrice`) with exactly three refusal reasons (`condition` · `no_price` · `none_available`); **there is no fourth, and none of the four can express *not yet mature*.** **It is not a fall-through: `deriveStatus(onHand)` (`:141-143`) returns `'available'` for any qty > 0 and `DERIVED_STATUSES` is `['available','depleted']` (`:138`) — the platform COMPUTES AND WRITES `status:'available'` onto a block six months from sellable.** ⚠️ **And this answers half the story's own `NEEDS:` question at code level, reported not decided:** the only escape hatch is `MANUAL_CONDITION_STATUSES = ['damaged','returned','archived']` (`:131`), and a `status` value for UNDER PRODUCTION would be a **THIRD author on the column tech-debt #71 already has two authors on** — the file's own comment (`:133-137`) says derived values are *"Never manually settable: the next qty write recomputes them"*, and #71 records that the qty-derive **already reverts a manual `archived`.** A clock derived from (potted date + rung maturation) cannot be clobbered that way; a status value can. ✏️ Refusal order is condition → price → quantity (`:166-168`), so a condition-modelled under-production would refuse *before* the price check — build-relevant, not a defect. **David rules; nothing added to the lot-status vocabulary here.**

- `BusinessInventory.tsx` UI — INSERT form (name, sku, qty, unit_cost, location, status, serial_number, cost_confidence, received_at, notes; receipt_id intentionally absent — linked by receipt flow) + **editable DATASHEET grid** at `/inventory` (board 5.1: create+read+**EDIT**, ledger #75, 2026-07-01). Commit b924800 (list) → **datasheet v1** `c2d34bf`. **The list is now an editable, sortable, filterable, column-hideable desktop grid** — hand-rolled column-descriptor spine (no grid lib): header-click sort, global search, status quick-filter, show/hide-columns menu; inline per-row edit (`TextCell`/`NumberCell`/`AmountCell` blur-commit + `<select>` enums; description in a row-expand) with **immediate UPDATE `.eq('id',row.id).eq('business_id',businessId)` — UUID-keyed, never sku-keyed** (`sku` DISPLAY-ONLY; Case 1/3, AC-2/3). Manual `unit_cost` → `cost_confidence=CONFIRMED` (overridable badge), clearing → UNKNOWN; Add-Item form aligned to the same rule. **Dup-size amber flag** reuses `findDuplicateSizeGroups` from the new leaf `@trace/shared/discovery/dupSize` — flags `(variant_group,size)` collisions inline (detect only; fix = edit the size/variant_group cell) → closes the CASE-5 loop at the reconcile surface. Inherits the `/inventory` view_costs route gate + existing RLS (no new perm). `[TRACE:invsheet]` ON. Reconcile surface = desktop-primary (capture=mobile principle).
